Top Songs of 2020

The top songs of 2020 reflected a year of musical innovation, genre-blending, and global viral hits. With pop, hip-hop, and R&B dominating the charts, artists like The Weeknd, Dua Lipa, BTS, and Doja Cat shaped the sound of the year. Despite the challenges of 2020, music became a much-needed escape, as streaming platforms and social media—especially TikTok—played a massive role in elevating breakout hits and reviving classics.

With a mix of club anthems, emotional ballads, and genre-defying collaborations, the 2020 top songs defined a new era of music consumption. Whether you’re rediscovering the best songs of 2020 or exploring the biggest 2020 hits for the first time, this archive provides the complete weekly Top 40 rankings from one of the most unique and transformative years in modern music history.

How We Ranked the Top Songs from 1966

*Note: Based on the Nolan Method, these are the year-end songs that represented the top songs of 2020 during the calendar year 2020. The Nolan Method ranking system was formulated by lead Top40Weekly contributor Jarrett Nolan and is based on a point system for an artist’s chart performance on Billboard charts.

Notable Songs & Artists from 2020

With pop ruling the airwaves, hip-hop and R&B dominating streaming, and K-pop breaking through global barriers, the top songs from 2020 not only defined a year of transition but also reimagined how fans connect with music in the digital age.

The Weeknd’s Record-Breaking Year

The Weeknd delivered one of the most dominant tracks of the decade with “Blinding Lights”. The song’s retro-inspired production and addictive melody propelled it to global success, breaking records for the longest run in Billboard Hot 100 history. Its ‘80s synth-pop revival sound influenced countless other releases, ushering in a wave of nostalgic pop into the mainstream.

The Rise of TikTok & Viral Hits

TikTok became the year’s most powerful music discovery platform, turning several tracks into global anthems. Doja Cat’s “Say So” became a viral sensation thanks to a dance challenge, while Megan Thee Stallion’s “Savage”—and its remix featuring Beyoncé—cemented her place in mainstream hip-hop. Even Fleetwood Mac’s “Dreams” reentered the charts after a viral skateboarding video, proving TikTok’s ability to resurrect decades-old classics alongside trending hits.

Pop, K-Pop & Global Superstars

2020 marked a breakthrough year for international pop. BTS made history with “Dynamite”, their first English-language single, which debuted at No. 1 on the Billboard Hot 100. The song brought K-pop into even broader Western mainstream recognition. Meanwhile, Dua Lipa’s Future Nostalgia album delivered modern disco revival with hits like “Don’t Start Now” and “Levitating”, redefining her as a global pop powerhouse.

Hip-Hop & R&B Take Over Streaming Platforms

Hip-hop and R&B continued to dominate digital streaming. Roddy Ricch’s “The Box” topped charts for multiple weeks with its signature “eee-err” intro becoming instantly recognizable. Drake’s “Toosie Slide” was built for TikTok success, while Lil Baby’s “The Bigger Picture” stood out as one of the year’s most powerful tracks, addressing the Black Lives Matter movement and resonating deeply across generations.

More 2020 Pop Songs
